==== examples/booleans.kj
string s0 = "ready"
string s1 = "empty"
string s2 = "not empty"
global g0: Int // count
global g1: Bool // ready
global g2: Bool // empty
global g3: Int // attempts

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Str
    local l1: Bool
    local l2: Bool
    local l3: Bool
    local l4: Bool
    local l5: Bool
    local l6: Bool
    local l7: Bool
    local l8: Bool
    local l9: Int
    local l10: Str
  b0:
    g0 = 0
    branch true, b1, b2
  b1:
    g0 = 1
    goto b2
  b2:
    l0 = IntToStr g0
    print l0
    l1 = IntCompare(Greater) g0, 0
    g1 = l1
    l2 = IntCompare(Equal) g0, 0
    g2 = l2
    l3 = g1
    branch l3, b3, b4
  b3:
    l4 = Not g2
    l3 = l4
    goto b4
  b4:
    branch l3, b5, b6
  b5:
    print s0
    goto b6
  b6:
    l5 = g2
    branch l5, b8, b7
  b7:
    l5 = false
    goto b8
  b8:
    branch l5, b9, b10
  b9:
    print s1
    goto b11
  b10:
    print s2
    goto b11
  b11:
    g3 = 0
    goto b12
  b12:
    l7 = IntCompare(Less) g3, 3
    l6 = l7
    branch l6, b15, b16
  b13:
    l9 = IntAdd g3, 1
    g3 = l9
    goto b12
  b14:
    l10 = IntToStr g3
    print l10
    return
  b15:
    l8 = Not g2
    l6 = l8
    goto b16
  b16:
    branch l6, b13, b14

==== examples/condition.kj

  b0:

    l0 = IntCompare(GreaterEqual) g0, 5
    branch l0, b1, b2
  b1:
    print s0
    goto b3
  b2:
    print s1
    goto b3
  b3:
    return
==== examples/functions.kj
record r0 environment
    {} ()
global g0: Function([Int], Int) // double

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Function([Int], Int)
    local l1: Int
    local l2: Str
    local l3: Int
    local l4: Str
  b0:
    l0 = closure f2
    g0 = l0
    l1 = call f1(5)
    l2 = IntToStr l1
    print l2
    l3 = call g0(21)
    l4 = IntToStr l3
    print l4
    return

function f1 factorial(l0: Int): Int
    local l1: Bool
    local l2: Int
    local l3: Int
    local l4: Int
  b0:
    l1 = IntCompare(LessEqual) l0, 1
    branch l1, b1, b2
  b1:
    return 1
  b2:
    l2 = IntSub l0, 1
    l3 = call f1(l2)
    l4 = IntMul l0, l3
    return l4

function f2 anonymous function(l0: Record(0), l1: Int): Int
    local l2: Int
  b0:
    l2 = IntMul l1, 2
    return l2

==== examples/input.kj
string s0 = "What's your name?"
string s1 = "Hello, "
string s2 = "!"
string s3 = "No input"
record r0 Option[string]
    Some (value: Str)
    None ()
global g0: Record(0) // name

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Record(0)
    local l1: Record(0)
    local l2: Int
    local l3: Bool
    local l4: Str
    local l5: Str // line
    local l6: Str
    local l7: Str
    local l8: Int
    local l9: Bool
  b0:
    l0 = koji_std_input([Value(Str(0)), Record(0), Location(1)])
    g0 = l0
    l1 = g0
    l2 = RecordTag l1
    l3 = IntCompare(Equal) l2, 0
    branch l3, b3, b2
  b1:
    return
  b2:
    l8 = RecordTag l1
    l9 = IntCompare(Equal) l8, 1
    branch l9, b5, b4
  b3:
    l4 = l1.0
    l5 = l4
    l6 = StrConcat s1, l5
    l7 = StrConcat l6, s2
    print l7
    goto b1
  b4:
    unreachable
  b5:
    print s3
    goto b1

global g0: Bool // lib/calc.kj initialized

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Int
    local l1: Str
    local l2: Decimal

    local l14: Str
  b0:
    call f1()
    l0 = call f2(2, 3)
    l1 = IntToStr l0
    print l1

    l3 = koji_std_math_sqrt_decimal([Value(Local(2)), Record(1)])

    l5 = RecordTag l4
    l6 = IntCompare(Equal) l5, 0
    branch l6, b3, b2
  b1:

  b2:

    branch l11, b5, b4

    l13 = l12
    l14 = RecordToStr l13
    print l14
    goto b1

  b0:
    branch g0, b1, b2
  b1:
    return
  b2:
    g0 = true
    return

  b0:

    return l2
==== examples/shapes.kj
string s0 = ": "
record r0 Shape
    Circle (radius: Decimal)
    Rectangle (width: Decimal, height: Decimal)
global g0: List(Record(0)) // shapes

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Record(0)
    local l1: Record(0)
    local l2: List(Record(0))
    local l3: List(Record(0))
    local l4: Int
    local l5: Int
    local l6: Bool
    local l7: Int
    local l8: Record(0) // shape
    local l9: Str
    local l10: Str
    local l11: Decimal
    local l12: Str
    local l13: Str
  b0:
    l0 = record variant 0 (1e0)
    l1 = record variant 1 (2e0, 3e0)
    l2 = list [l0, l1]
    g0 = l2
    l3 = g0
    l4 = ListLength l3
    l5 = 0
    goto b1
  b1:
    l6 = IntCompare(Less) l5, l4
    branch l6, b2, b4
  b2:
    l8 = l3[l5]
    l9 = RecordToStr l8
    l10 = StrConcat l9, s0
    l11 = call f1(l8)
    l12 = DecimalToStr l11
    l13 = StrConcat l10, l12
    print l13
    goto b3
  b3:
    l7 = IntAdd l5, 1
    l5 = l7
    goto b1
  b4:
    return

function f1 area(l0: Record(0)): Decimal
    local l1: Decimal
    local l2: Record(0)
    local l3: Int
    local l4: Bool
    local l5: Decimal
    local l6: Decimal // radius
    local l7: Decimal
    local l8: Decimal
    local l9: Int
    local l10: Bool
    local l11: Decimal
    local l12: Decimal // width
    local l13: Decimal
    local l14: Decimal // height
    local l15: Decimal
  b0:
    l2 = l0
    l3 = RecordTag l2
    l4 = IntCompare(Equal) l3, 0
    branch l4, b3, b2
  b1:
    return l1
  b2:
    l9 = RecordTag l2
    l10 = IntCompare(Equal) l9, 1
    branch l10, b5, b4
  b3:
    l5 = l2.0
    l6 = l5
    l7 = DecimalMul 314e-2, l6
    l8 = DecimalMul l7, l6
    l1 = l8
    goto b1
  b4:
    unreachable
  b5:
    l11 = l2.0
    l12 = l11
    l13 = l2.1
    l14 = l13
    l15 = DecimalMul l12, l14
    l1 = l15
    goto b1
==== examples/types.kj
string s0 = "hello"
string s1 = "olá"
string s2 = "world"

string s4 = "Pen"
string s5 = "empty"
string s6 = "small"
string s7 = "large"
string s8 = " ("
string s9 = ") - "
record r0 Product
    {} (id: Int, name: Str, price: Decimal)
global g0: Map(Str, Str) // translations
global g1: Str // label

function f0 main(): Void
    local l0: Map(Str, Str)
    local l1: Int
    local l2: Str
    local l3: Decimal
    local l4: Record(0)
    local l5: Str
    local l6: Str
    local l7: Str
    local l8: Int
    local l9: Int
    local l10: Bool
    local l11: Int // n
    local l12: Bool
  b0:
    l0 = map [s0: s1, s2: s3]
    g0 = l0
    l1 = 1
    l2 = s4
    l3 = 25e-1
    l4 = record variant 0 (l1, l2, l3)
    l5 = call f1(l4)
    print l5
    l6 = g0{s0}
    print l6
    l8 = MapLength g0
    l9 = l8
    l10 = IntCompare(Equal) l9, 0
    branch l10, b3, b2
  b1:
    g1 = l7
    print g1
    return
  b2:
    l11 = l9
    l12 = IntCompare(Less) l11, 3
    branch l12, b5, b4
  b3:
    l7 = s5
    goto b1
  b4:
    l7 = s7
    goto b1
  b5:
    l7 = s6
    goto b1
  b6:
    unreachable

function f1 describe(l0: Record(0)): Str
    local l1: Str
    local l2: Str
    local l3: Int
    local l4: Str
    local l5: Str
    local l6: Str
    local l7: Decimal
    local l8: Str
    local l9: Str
  b0:
    l1 = l0.1
    l2 = StrConcat l1, s8
    l3 = l0.0
    l4 = IntToStr l3
    l5 = StrConcat l2, l4
    l6 = StrConcat l5, s9
    l7 = l0.2
    l8 = DecimalToStr l7
    l9 = StrConcat l6, l8
    return l9
